Compute HHI and FA

2026 06 08 17:13

What this file does (chunk summary)

  1. {setup} — Load libraries, define helpers (grepout, geovars, GeoLevel)
  2. {read irp5 file} — Load irp5.qs
  3. {fraction affected for all years} — Loop yr 8–22: compute duration, monthly income, SubMW, FA/FAe/FAMP/FAeMP at firm and establishment levels. Save FA{yr}.qs, AggregateSummary.qs
  4. {create FA panel} — Stack yearly FA → FAD.qs
  5. {industry code for ag} (eval=F) — One-time: classify agriculture firms → TaxrefnoOfAgri.qs
  6. {faa and Fadata are first lines of FAD in each year} — Collapse to establishment-year, filter duration and agriculture → FAOfAgri.qs, fadataUniqueEntries.qs
  7. {copy FAD and derived files to saveddata} (eval=F) — Copy outputs
  8. {descriptive stats FA and jobs} — Tables of aggregate FA (all industries + agriculture)
  9. {plot FAs by size} — Density of FA by firm size
  10. {plot FAs by jobs employment} — Density of FA in jobs vs employment
  11. {plot number of agri jobs taxyear 2012-2020} — Histogram of ag establishment size
  12. {agri Jobs by firm age} (eval=F) — Bar chart: ag jobs by incumbents vs entrants
  13. {plot FA in agri 2012-2020} — Density of FA by tax year
  14. {plot FA of agri} — Boxplot of FA by tax year, Exposed/Unexposed
  15. {define irp5M by location granularity} — Classify location completeness, create irp5M/L/D/P panels, filter agriculture → irp5Ma.qs
  16. {copy irp5M and derived files to saveddata} (eval=F) — Copy outputs
  17. {hhi} — Compute labour market shares, HHI, nHHI at 4 geo levels. Save ShareHHI{yr}.qs. Plot HHI 2012 vs 2013
  18. {create LShare panel} — Stack yearly HHI → LShareHHI.qs
  19. {LSa is LS of agri only} — Filter HHI to agriculture → HHIAgriRows.qs
  20. {copy LShareHHI.qs HHIAgriRows.qs to saveddata} (eval=F) — Copy outputs
  21. {further checks on shares} (eval=F) — Ad hoc checks (stale column names)
  22. {plot HHI by year} (eval=F) — Density of HHI by year

What we do in the project

  1. Create merged panel using stata: “Data file”
    • Use the same definitions as Michael’s codes
    • But include imp_mic_sic7_3d == 16 industry
    • Done in other do file
    • Save Data file in tsv format to read in R
  2. Compute HHI using CIT-IRP5 at various levels: “HHI file”
    • Use CIT-IRP5_2012 HHI in 2012
    • Verify if counts are not erroneous
  3. “Analysis file” = Data file (panel 2009-2022) + HHI file
    • Merge at various levels
    • Need main place level analysis for larger statistical power
  4. Descriptive statistic
  5. Hypothesis testing
    • % Employment change = a + b1FA + b2HHI + b3*(FA*HHI) + FEs + e
    • Test b3 > 0 labor market power mitigates disemployment impacts
    • Expect b1<0 or b1 = 0
    • No prior for the sign of b2

grepout <- function(str, x)

  # returns element of match (not numbers)

  x[grep(str, x, perl = T)]

table0 <- function(x) table(x, useNA = "ifany")

#### mask_dots: replace every char except positions in `keep` with ".".

#### Used to anonymise taxrefno / uid in display-only prints (not in keys or joins).

mask_dots <- function(x, keep = c(3, 6, 7, 8, 11)) {

  x <- as.character(x)

  n <- nchar(x)

  out <- strrep(".", n)

  for (k in keep) {

    m <- !is.na(x) & n >= k

    substr(out[m], k, k) <- substr(x[m], k, k)

  }

  out

}

read.tablev <- function(fn, quotemark = "", headertrue = TRUE, 

  blskip = TRUE, mat = F, ...) 

{

  x <- read.table(fn, sep = "\t", header = headertrue, blank.lines.skip = blskip, 

        quote = quotemark, na.strings = "", ...)

  if (is.null(dim(x)) | nrow(x) == 1) 

  {

    cn <- names(x)

    dim(x) <- c(1, length(x))

    colnames(x) <- cn

  }

  if (mat) x <- as.matrix(x)

  return(x)

}

write.tablev <- function(x, fn, colnamestrue = TRUE, rownamestrue = FALSE, 

nastrings = "", ...) 

  write.table(x, fn, sep = "\t", quote = FALSE, 

        row.names = rownamestrue, col.names = colnamestrue, na = nastrings, ...)

GeoLevel <- c("Prv", "Dis", "Loc", "Mai")

geovars <- c("busprov_geo", "busdistmuni_geo", 

  "buslocmuni_geo", "busmainplc_geo")
  • FA does not require location info in theory, but it does in practice
    • If the establishment ID payereferenceno is not corrupt, location info is not necessary
    • But we cannot rely on payereferenceno, because establishments at different locations share the same payereferenceno, probably firms and SARS did not care to assign unique values for each establishments
    • So we must rely on location info to aggregate employees/jobs at the establishment level of a specific location
  • HHI requires location info, so we use irp5M that have information of Main Place
    • Whenever location information is updated, we need to compute HHI
    • To update location information, need to update irp5.qs → irp5M.qs → LShareHHI.qs

Fraction affected

  1. Use IRP5 to count total number of workers, number of affected, FA = affected/total for all years

    • PropToMW := IncomeMonth/2274: income/MW ratio
    • SubMW := as.integer(PropToMW < 1 & IncomeMonth > 0)

    Firm level: by = .(Txrf, taxyear)

    Establishment level: by = c(“Txrf”, geovars, “taxyear”)

    • NumSubMW, NumSubMWMP := as.integer(sum(SubMW==1L))
    • Jobs, JobsMP := as.integer(sum(IncomeMonth > 0))
    • NumSubMWe, NumSubMWeMP := as.integer(uniqueN(UID[SubMW==1L]))
    • Employees, EmployeesMP := as.integer(uniqueN(UID[IncomeMonth > 0]))
    • FA, FAMP := round(NumSubMW/Jobs, 6)]
    • FAe, FAeMP := round(NumSubMWe/Employees, 6)]

  2. Need to use unfiltered data like irp5.qs (except for NatureOfPer == A and empty taxrefno)

    • Entries of irp5 look naturally balanced across years
    • Many NAs in location info in early years ⇒ loss of sample in early years
if (exists("irp5gi"))    rm(irp5gi)    #### CLAUDE exi: exists() for exact names, ls(pattern=) for partial/regex

if (exists("irp5gir"))   rm(irp5gir)  #### CLAUDE exi:

if (exists("irp5Clean")) rm(irp5Clean) #### CLAUDE exi:

#### irp5.qs is created in IRP5Condense.rmd

library(qs)

library(data.table)

irp5 <- qread(paste0(pathsaveddata, "irp5.qs"), 

  nthreads = DTThreads, use_alt_rep = TRUE)


library(fasttime)

#### Takes 20-30 mins: Start  ####

#### irp5M <- qread(paste0(pathdata, "irp5M.qs"), nthreads = 13)

#### ipyrc0 <- irp5M[

#### !duplicated(irp5M[, .(busmainplc_geo, taxrefno, UID, 

#### DateBirth, DateStart, DateEnd,

#### kerr_income, ptrs_income, a3601_income)]), 

#### ]

#### Takes 20-30 mins: End  ####

RawCols <- c(

  "taxyear",

  "busprov_geo", "busdistmuni_geo", "buslocmuni_geo", "busmainplc_geo",

  "taxrefno", "Txrf", "UID", "uid",

  "DateStart", "DateEnd",

  "a3601_income", "kerr_income", "ptrs_income"

)

NeededCols <- c(

  setdiff(RawCols, c("taxyear", "DateStart", "DateEnd", "kerr_income", "ptrs_income")),

  "DurationMonth", "DurMonthMarch",

  "IncomeMonth", "NumJobsPerWorker",

  "PropToMW", "SubMW",

  "Jobs", "NumSubMW", "Employees", "NumSubMWe",

  "JobsMP", "NumSubMWMP", "EmployeesMP", "NumSubMWeMP",

  "JobsPerWorker",

  "FA", "FAe", "FAMP", "FAeMP"

)

aggsummary <- FAD <- NULL

for (yr in 8:22) {

  ipyrc <- irp5[taxyear == 2000+yr, ..RawCols]#[1:100000, ]

  #### Compute job duration and monthly income

  ####   (DateEnd-DateStart2012)/30.5

  ####   a3601_income/DurationMonth

  ipyrc[, DateStart20yr := DateStart]

  #### If DateStart is before start of taxyear, March 1 

  ####  ==> DateStart20yr = March 1 of taxyear-1

  ipyrc[DateStart < fastPOSIXct(paste0(2000+yr-1, "/03/01")),

          DateStart20yr := fastPOSIXct(paste0(2000+yr-1,"/03/01"))]

  #### IncomeMonth = monthly income, duration is measured as the same taxyear

  ipyrc[, DurationMonth := round(as.numeric((DateEnd-DateStart)/30.5), 3)]

  ipyrc[, DurMonthMarch := round(as.numeric((DateEnd-DateStart20yr)/30.5), 3)]

  #### DurationMonth can be zero if work is only for 1 day

  ipyrc[DateStart == DateEnd, c("DurationMonth", "DurMonthMarch") := round(1/30.5, 3)]

  lapply(ipyrc[, .(DurationMonth, DurMonthMarch)], 

    quantile, c(seq(0, 1, .25)[-5], .985, .995, 1), na.rm = T)

  #### DateStart too old for some rows (typo for previous year or actual starting dates)  

  #### DurationMonth == DurMonthMarch for these entries

  #### Threshold is set to 20 because typo for prev Ma 1 will give DurationMonth==24

  dim(ipyrc[DurationMonth > 20, 

      .(Txrf, DateStart, DateStart20yr, DateEnd, DurationMonth, DurMonthMarch)])

  ipyrc[DurationMonth > 20, DurationMonth := DurMonthMarch]

  #### typos (because we corrected for revision reporting for previous years)

  dim(ipyrc[DurMonthMarch > 100, 

    .(Txrf, DateStart, DateStart20yr, DateEnd, DurationMonth, DurMonthMarch)])

  ipyrc[, IncomeMonth   := round(a3601_income/DurationMonth, 0)]

  setkey(ipyrc, busmainplc_geo, taxrefno, UID, DateStart)

  #### DateStart2, DateEnd2: Dates for 2nd job in the same firm

  ipyrc[, DateStart2 := shift(DateStart, n = 1L, type = "lead"), by = .(Txrf, taxyear, UID)]

  ipyrc[,   DateEnd2 := shift(DateEnd, n = 1L, type = "lead"), by = .(Txrf, taxyear, UID)]

  ipyrc[, c("TDurationMonth", "DJobDurationMonth") := 0]

  #### If job2 after job1 ends: 

  #### Double work duration: DateEnd  - DateStart2

  ####  Total work duration: DateEnd2 - DateStart20yr

  ####  <----job1---->

  ####           <----job2---->

  ####  DateStart20yr < DateStart2

  ####  DateEnd < DateEnd2

  ####  DateEnd > DateStart2

  ipyrc[DateStart20yr < DateStart2 & DateEnd < DateEnd2 & DateEnd > DateStart2, 

    DJobDurationMonth := round(as.numeric((DateEnd - DateStart2)/30.5), 3)]

    #### Negative TDurationMonth for 35 entries for 2012 

    #### because their reported DateEnd2 is before 2011/03/01

  ipyrc[DateStart20yr < DateStart2 & DateEnd < DateEnd2 & DateEnd > DateStart2, 

    TDurationMonth := round(as.numeric((DateEnd2 - DateStart20yr)/30.5), 3)]

  #### If job2 ends before job1 ends: 

  #### Double work duration: DateEnd2 - DateStart2

  ####  Total work duration: DateEnd  - DateStart20yr

  ####  <----------job1---------->

  ####           <----job2---->

  ####  DateStart20yr < DateStart2

  ####  DateEnd > DateEnd2

  ####     (DateEnd2 - DateStart2   )/30.5 (double work duration)

  ####     (DateEnd  - DateStart20yr)/30.5 (job1+job2 duration)

  ipyrc[DateStart20yr < DateStart2 & DateEnd > DateEnd2, 

         DJobDurationMonth := round(as.numeric((DateEnd2 - DateStart2)/30.5), 3)]

  ipyrc[DateStart20yr < DateStart2 & DateEnd > DateEnd2, 

         TDurationMonth    := round(as.numeric((DateEnd - DateStart20yr)/30.5), 3)]

  #### TDurationMonth = Total duration of jobs

  ipyrc[, TDurationMonth   := TDurationMonth[TDurationMonth == max(TDurationMonth, na.rm = T)][1],

    by = .(Txrf, taxyear, UID)]

  ipyrc[, DoubleJobRatio   := round((DJobDurationMonth/TDurationMonth)*100, 2)]

  ipyrc[, DoubleJobRatio   := DoubleJobRatio[!is.na(DoubleJobRatio)][1],by = .(Txrf, taxyear, UID)]

  ipyrc[, NumJobsPerWorker := as.integer(.N), by = .(Txrf, taxyear, UID)]

  #### TIncomeMonth = sum of monthly income across jobs

  ipyrc[, TIncomeMonth     := sum(IncomeMonth, na.rm = T), by = .(Txrf, taxyear, UID)]

  #### check data

  summary(ipyrc[busmainplc_geo != "" & Txrf != "" & NumJobsPerWorker > 1, 

    .(busmainplc_geo, Txrf, UID, uid, NumJobsPerWorker, 

      DateStart, DateEnd,

      DateStart2, DateEnd2, DurationMonth,DurMonthMarch, 

      DJobDurationMonth, TDurationMonth, DoubleJobRatio,

      a3601_income, 

      kerr_income, IncomeMonth, TIncomeMonth)])

  dim(ipyrc[NumJobsPerWorker > 10, 

    .(busmainplc_geo, Txrf, UID, uid, NumJobsPerWorker, 

      DateStart, DateEnd,

      DateStart2, DateEnd2, DurationMonth, 

      DJobDurationMonth, TDurationMonth, DoubleJobRatio,

      a3601_income, IncomeMonth, TIncomeMonth)])

  ipyrc[busmainplc_geo != "" & Txrf != "", 

    .(busmainplc_geo, taxrefno, DateStart, DateEnd, 

      DurationMonth, a3601_income, IncomeMonth)]

  #### Find sub MW workers and define fraction affected

  #### Sums (Jobs, NumSubMW, etc.) counts total in two levels

  ####   a. Firm (Txrf):                good: more obs, bad: not granular

  ####   b. Establishment (Main Place): good: granular, bad: fewer obs

  #### We can use IncomeMonth or TIncomeMonth

  ####   PropToMW: Proportion to MW line = IncomeMonth/2274 ==> SubMW is wage per job

  ####      SubMW: PropToMW < 1 & IncomeMonth > 0

  ####  NumSubMW : number of entries with SubMW == 1L

  ####  NumSubMWe: number of unique UID entries with SubMW == 1L

  ####       Jobs: number of entries with IncomeMonth > 0

  ####  Employees: number of unique UID with IncomeMonth > 0

  ####        FA : NumSubMW/Jobs (FA in jobs)

  ####        FAe: NumSubMWe/Employees (FA in employment)

  #### Note: uid is NA in some entries, UID is not 

  ipyrc[, PropToMW  := round(IncomeMonth/2274, 6)]

  ipyrc[, SubMW     := as.integer(PropToMW < 1 & IncomeMonth > 0)]

  #### a. Firm level

  ipyrc[, NumSubMW  := as.integer(sum(SubMW==1L)), 

    by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

  ipyrc[, Jobs      := as.integer(sum(IncomeMonth > 0)), 

    by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

  ipyrc[, NumSubMWe := as.integer(uniqueN(UID[SubMW==1L])), 

    by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

  ipyrc[, Employees := as.integer(uniqueN(UID[IncomeMonth > 0])), 

    by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

  #### b. Establishment (Main Place) level

  ipyrc[, NumSubMWMP  := as.integer(sum(SubMW==1L)), 

    by = .(Txrf, busprov_geo, busdistmuni_geo, 

      buslocmuni_geo, busmainplc_geo, taxyear)]

  ipyrc[, JobsMP      := as.integer(sum(IncomeMonth > 0)), 

    by = .(Txrf, busprov_geo, busdistmuni_geo, 

      buslocmuni_geo, busmainplc_geo, taxyear)]

  ipyrc[, NumSubMWeMP := as.integer(uniqueN(UID[SubMW==1L])), 

    by = .(Txrf, busprov_geo, busdistmuni_geo, 

      buslocmuni_geo, busmainplc_geo, taxyear)]

  ipyrc[, EmployeesMP := as.integer(uniqueN(UID[IncomeMonth > 0])), 

    by = .(Txrf, busprov_geo, busdistmuni_geo, 

      buslocmuni_geo, busmainplc_geo, taxyear)]

  ipyrc[, FA     := round(NumSubMW/Jobs, 6)]

  ipyrc[, FAe    := round(NumSubMWe/Employees, 6)]

  ipyrc[, FAMP   := round(NumSubMWMP/JobsMP, 6)]

  ipyrc[, FAeMP  := round(NumSubMWeMP/EmployeesMP, 6)]

  ####ipyrc[grepl("^BCJXBCC", Txrf), 

  ####  .(busmainplc_geo, taxyear, 

  ####    Txrf, uid, UID, DateStart, DurationMonth, 

  ####    IncomeMonth, SubMW, NumSubMW, Jobs, FA,

  ####    NumSubMWe, Employees, FAe)]

  #### Fin year 2013 = 2012 Mar 01 - 2013 Feb 29

  ####  <==> 0L for DateEnd < 2012 Mar 01 or DateStart >= 2013 Mar 01 

  #### FinYr20yr := 1L ==> DateStart & DateEnd are correct for current taxyear

  ipyrc[, FinYr20yr := 1L]

  ipyrc[

    DateEnd    < fastPOSIXct(paste0(2000+(yr-1), "/03/01")) |

    DateStart >= fastPOSIXct(paste0(2000+yr,     "/03/01")), 

    FinYr20yr := 0L]

  ipyrc[, JobsPerWorker := .N, by = c(geovars, "taxrefno", "uid")]

  #### Select variables to keep

  FAdata <- ipyrc[, ..NeededCols]

  #### summary(FAdata)

  #### summary(FAdata[is.na(FA), .(FA, Jobs, a3601_income, IncomeMonth, DurationMonth)])

  FAdata[, Size := "micro"]

  FAdata[Employees >= 10 & Employees <= 49, Size := "small"]

  FAdata[Employees >= 50 & Employees <= 249, Size := "medium"]

  FAdata[Employees >= 250, Size := "large"]

  FAdata[is.na(Employees) | Employees == 0, Size := NA]

  FAdata[, Size := factor(Size, levels = c("micro", "small", "medium", "large"))]

  #### Number of affected workers in aggregates

  #### ag1 <- ipyrc[, .(

  ####     JobsPerWorker

  #### CLAUDE tpo: bare column in aggregate j (no by=) — data.table returns full vector,

  ####   scalars recycled to match; ag1 gets N rows/year instead of 1, inflating aggsummary.

  ag1 <- ipyrc[, .(

      MeanJobsPerWorker = mean(JobsPerWorker, na.rm = TRUE)

    , TotalEmployees = uniqueN(UID[IncomeMonth > 0])

    , TotalSubMWWorkers = uniqueN(UID[IncomeMonth > 0 & SubMW==1L])

    , TotalFirmsWithEmployees = uniqueN(taxrefno[IncomeMonth > 0])

    , TotalAffectedFirms = uniqueN(taxrefno[IncomeMonth > 0 & SubMW==1L])

    )

    ][, .(

      MeanJobsPerWorker, TotalEmployees, TotalSubMWWorkers,

      AggFA = round((TotalSubMWWorkers/TotalEmployees)*100, 2),

      TotalFirmsWithEmployees, TotalAffectedFirms,

      AggFirmFA = round((TotalAffectedFirms/TotalFirmsWithEmployees)*100, 2)

    )]

  ag1[, taxyear := 2000+yr]

  aggsummary <- rbindlist(list(aggsummary, ag1), use.names = T, fill = T)

  ag2 <- ipyrc[, .(

      TotalEmployees = uniqueN(UID[IncomeMonth > 0]) 

    , TotalSubMWWorkers = uniqueN(UID[IncomeMonth > 0 & SubMW==1L]) 

    , TotalFirmsWithEmployees = uniqueN(taxrefno[IncomeMonth > 0]) 

    , TotalAffectedFirms = uniqueN(taxrefno[IncomeMonth > 0 & SubMW==1L]) 

    ), 

    by = .(taxyear, busprov_geo)

    ][, .(

      busprov_geo, TotalEmployees, TotalSubMWWorkers, 

      AggFA = round((TotalSubMWWorkers/TotalEmployees)*100, 2),

      TotalFirmsWithEmployees, TotalAffectedFirms,

      AggFirmFA = round((TotalAffectedFirms/TotalFirmsWithEmployees)*100, 2)

    )]

  ag2[, taxyear := 2000+yr]

  aggsummary <- rbindlist(list(aggsummary, ag2), use.names = T, fill = T)

  FAdata[, taxyear := 2000+yr]

  if (yr < 10) yr <- paste0("0", yr)

  qsave(FAdata, paste0(pathdata, "FA", yr, ".qs"), nthreads = DTThreads)

  FAD <- rbindlist(list(FAD, FAdata))

}

aggsummary <- qread(paste0(pathdata, "AggregateSummary.qs"),

  nthreads = DTThreads, use_alt_rep = TRUE)

  1. For each taxyear, FAdata has 4 indicators of “Fraction Affected”:

    • FA: At firm level, computed with jobs
    • FAe: At firm level, computed with individuals
    • FAMP: At establishment (Main Place) level, computed with jobs
    • FAeMP: At establishment (Main Place) level, computed with individuals

    Accordingly, we have created for FA

    • Jobs: Number of jobs
    • NumSubMW: Number of sub MW jobs
    • Employees: Number of employees
    • NumSubMWe: Number of sub MW employees

    Also for FAe, FAMP, FAeMP, we have created these four variables

  2. We append FAdata of each taxyear → FAD (FA panel at individual level)

  3. We select rows of FAD as

    • Fadata: FAFirstRow.qs, establishment level FA panel (1st rows of FAD by location-taxrefno-taxyear)
    • fadatai: FAAndJobs.qs, duration within 1 year of Fadata + industry
    • `faa`: FAOfAgri.qs, fadatai[Crop==1L|Animal==1L|Forest==1L, ]
    • estfa: fadataUniqueEntries.qs, unique entries by location-taxrefno-taxyear in fadatai (almost same as fadatai except uniqueness is explicitly imposed)

      TotalJobs               = sum(TotalEmployees)

    , TotalSubMWJobs          = sum(TotalSubMWWorkers)

  ), by = taxyear][, .(

        taxyear, TotalJobs, TotalSubMWJobs, 

        TotalFirmsWithEmployees, TotalAffectedFirms,

  ]

#### Agriculture

faa <- qread(paste0(pathsaveddata, "FAOfAgri.qs"), 

  nthreads = DTThreads, use_alt_rep = TRUE) #### CLAUDE tpo:

agsum <- faa[, .(

      TotalJobs          = sum(JobsMP)

                         #### CLAUDE agg: wrong aggregation level

                         #### (firm vs establishment, row-level vs

                         #### pre-computed). Was sum(Jobs[...]),

                         #### firm-level overcounts

    , TotalSubMWJobs     = sum(NumSubMWMP)

                         #### CLAUDE agg: was uniqueN(Jobs[...]),

                         #### wrong aggregation

    , TotalEmployees     = sum(EmployeesMP)

                         #### CLAUDE agg: was uniqueN(UID[...]),

                         #### one UID per row

    , TotalSubMWWorkers  = sum(NumSubMWeMP)

                         #### CLAUDE agg: was uniqueN(UID[...]),

                         #### one UID per row

    , TotalFirmsWithEmp  = uniqueN(taxrefno[JobsMP > 0])

                         #### CLAUDE agg: was IncomeMonth > 0

    , TotalAffectedFirms = uniqueN(taxrefno[NumSubMWMP > 0])

                         #### CLAUDE agg: was SubMW==1L

                         #### (one worker's status) 

    ), 

    by = .(taxyear)

    ][, .(

      taxyear, 

      TotalJobs, TotalSubMWJobs, TotalEmployees, TotalSubMWWorkers, 

      AggFA = round((TotalSubMWJobs/TotalJobs)*100, 2),

      AggFAe = round((TotalSubMWWorkers/TotalEmployees)*100, 2),

      TotalFirmsWithEmp, TotalAffectedFirms,

      AggFirmFA = round((TotalAffectedFirms/TotalFirmsWithEmp)*100, 2)

    )]

Location information granularity checks

We need to know how granular the location information is. Based on granularity, we decide at what level one can use the sampled firm.

irp5 (all firms, no filter; used for FA)

├── irp5M             Main Place    NARowsMai==0 & LocGranularFirm==Mai  |  NARowsMai>0 & MainPlNum==1

├── irp5L  (not M)    Local Muni    NARowsLoc==0 & LocGranularFirm==Loc  |  NARowsLoc>0 & LocMunNum==1

├── irp5D  (not M/L)  Dist Muni     NARowsDis==0 & LocGranularFirm==Dis  |  NARowsDis>0 & DisMunNum==1

├── irp5P  (not M/L/D) Province     NARowsPrv==0 & LocGranularFirm==Prv  |  NARowsPrv>0 & ProvNum==1

└── (remainder)                     location ambiguous or absent at all levels

Mutually exclusive by construction; firm-level (all rows travel together). irp5 → FA; irp5M → HHI.

rm(FAD); rm(Fadata); rm(fadata); rm(inda); 

rm(faa)

gc()
used (Mb) gc trigger (Mb) max used (Mb)
Ncells 2.510706e+07 1.340900e+03 6.673239e+07 3.563900e+03 6.673239e+07 3.563900e+03
Vcells 7.025542e+09 5.360070e+04 2.190430e+10 1.671166e+05 1.822056e+10 1.390119e+05 
if (!exists("RunSep")) RunSep <- T

if (RunSep || length(ls(pattern="^irp5$"))==0)

  irp5 <- qread(paste0(pathsaveddata, "irp5.qs"), 

    nthreads = DTThreads, use_alt_rep = TRUE)

geoLevel <- c("Prv", "Dis", "Loc", "Mai")

geovars <- grepout("^bus.*geo$", colnames(irp5))

#### Location granularity indicator

#### irp5[, LocGranular := "none"]

#### CLAUDE opt: replaced 15-year × 4-level loop (60 full-table scans) with a single

####   fcase() pass grouped by (Txrf, taxyear). any() evaluates group-wide and fcase()

####   short-circuits; semantics identical — all rows of a qualifying (Txrf, yr) group

####   get the same level as before.

#### for (yr in 8:22) {

####   irp5[Txrf %in% Txrf

####     [busprov_geo != "" & busdistmuni_geo != "" &

####      buslocmuni_geo != "" & busmainplc_geo != "" & taxyear == 2000 + yr] &

####     taxyear == 2000 + yr, LocGranular := GeoLevel[4]]

####   irp5[Txrf %in% Txrf

####     [busprov_geo != "" & busdistmuni_geo != "" &

####      buslocmuni_geo != "" & busmainplc_geo == "" & taxyear == 2000 + yr] &

####     taxyear == 2000 + yr, LocGranular := GeoLevel[3]]

####   irp5[Txrf %in% Txrf

####     [busprov_geo != "" & busdistmuni_geo != "" &

####      buslocmuni_geo == "" & busmainplc_geo == "" & taxyear == 2000 + yr] &

####     taxyear == 2000 + yr, LocGranular := GeoLevel[2]]

####   irp5[Txrf %in% Txrf

####     [busprov_geo != "" & busdistmuni_geo == "" &

####      buslocmuni_geo == "" & busmainplc_geo == "" & taxyear == 2000 + yr] &

####     taxyear == 2000 + yr, LocGranular := GeoLevel[1]]

#### }

#### CLAUDE opt: pre-compute existence flags per (Txrf, taxyear); avoids

####   repeating the full any() condition 4x per level; fcase() then uses

####   simple logical ops with no by= (flags already per-row constants)

#### !is.na() is required: without it, a group with only NA/empty geo values

####   gives any(NA) = NA instead of FALSE, breaking fcase() assignment.

####   Trace for x <- c(NA, "", "") (no valid entry in group):

####   x != ""           → c(NA,    FALSE, FALSE)

####   !is.na(x)         → c(FALSE, TRUE,  TRUE)

####   so far            → c(FALSE, FALSE, FALSE)

####   !grepl("EXCEP",x) → c(NA,    TRUE,  TRUE)

####   element-wise &    → c(FALSE, FALSE, FALSE)

####   any(...)          → FALSE  ← CORRECT; without !is.na(): any(c(NA,F,F)) = NA

irp5[, c("provexists", "distexists", "locexists", "mainexists") := .(

  any(busprov_geo     != "" & !is.na(busprov_geo)     & !grepl("EXCEP", busprov_geo)),

  any(busdistmuni_geo != "" & !is.na(busdistmuni_geo) & !grepl("EXCEP", busdistmuni_geo)),

  any(buslocmuni_geo  != "" & !is.na(buslocmuni_geo)  & !grepl("EXCEP", buslocmuni_geo)),

  any(busmainplc_geo  != "" & !is.na(busmainplc_geo)  & !grepl("EXCEP", busmainplc_geo))

), by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

irp5[, LocGranular := fcase(

  #### Granular down to Main Place

   provexists &  distexists &  locexists &  mainexists, GeoLevel[4],

  #### Granular down only to Loc muni

   provexists &  distexists &  locexists & !mainexists, GeoLevel[3],

  #### Granular down only to Dist muni

   provexists &  distexists & !locexists & !mainexists, GeoLevel[2],

  #### Granular down only to Province

   provexists & !distexists & !locexists & !mainexists, GeoLevel[1],

  default = "none"

)]

#### LocGranular = Main, Loc, Dist, Prov are all exclusive

####   LocGranular == Main group has info of Prov 

####   but is not included in LocGranular = Prov group

irp5[, c("provexists", "distexists", "locexists", "mainexists") := NULL]

irp5[, LocGranular := factor(LocGranular, levels = c(GeoLevel, "none"))]

#### LocGranularFirm: Most granular location info (order sensitive)

#### LocGranularJob: Most granular location info (order insensitive)

setnames(irp5, "LocGranular", "LocGranularFirm")

irp5[, LocGranularJob := "none"]

irp5[busprov_geo != "", LocGranularJob := GeoLevel[1]]

irp5[busdistmuni_geo != "", LocGranularJob := GeoLevel[2]]

irp5[buslocmuni_geo != "", LocGranularJob := GeoLevel[3]]

irp5[busmainplc_geo != "", LocGranularJob := GeoLevel[4]]



#### Location granularity due to establishment clustering

#### When location info is missing in some rows, one can allocate workers

#### only up to location level where all establishments are clustered

for (mm in 1:length(GeoLevel)) {

  irp5[, (GeoLevel[mm]) := get(geovars[mm])]

  irp5[get(geovars[mm]) == "", (GeoLevel[mm]) := NA]

}

#### Number of unique entries at each level 

#### Subtract 1 because NA is counted as 1 (all NA entries are MainPlNum == 1L)

#### irp5[, MainPlNum := as.integer(length(unique(Mai)))-1L, by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

#### CLAUDE opt: uniqueN() is data.table-native; avoids length(unique()) overhead;

####   returns integer already so as.integer() wrapper dropped. -1L correction

####   unchanged: NA counts as 1 distinct value in both uniqueN() and unique().

irp5[, MainPlNum := uniqueN(Mai)-1L, by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

#### irp5[, LocMunNum := as.integer(length(unique(Loc)))-1L, by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

#### CLAUDE opt:

irp5[, LocMunNum := uniqueN(Loc)-1L, by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

#### irp5[, DisMunNum := as.integer(length(unique(Dis)))-1L, by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

#### CLAUDE opt:

irp5[, DisMunNum := uniqueN(Dis)-1L, by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

#### irp5[, ProvNum   := as.integer(length(unique(Prv)))-1L, by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

#### CLAUDE opt:

irp5[, ProvNum   := uniqueN(Prv)-1L, by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

irp5[, MainPlNA := as.integer(all(is.na(Mai))), by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

irp5[, LocMunNA := as.integer(all(is.na(Loc))), by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

irp5[, DisMunNA := as.integer(all(is.na(Dis))), by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

irp5[, ProvNA := as.integer(all(is.na(Prv))), by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]



irp5[, CommonLocality := "none"]

irp5[

      ProvNum == 1L & 

    DisMunNum == 1L & 

    LocMunNum == 1L & 

    MainPlNum == 1L &

    MainPlNA  == 0L, 

  CommonLocality := GeoLevel[4]]

irp5[

    ProvNum   == 1L & 

    DisMunNum == 1L & 

    LocMunNum == 1L & 

    MainPlNum != 1L & 

    LocMunNA  == 0L, 

  CommonLocality := GeoLevel[3]]

irp5[

    ProvNum   == 1L & 

    DisMunNum == 1L & 

    LocMunNum != 1L & 

    MainPlNum != 1L &

    DisMunNA  == 0L, 

  CommonLocality := GeoLevel[2]]

irp5[

    ProvNum   == 1L & 

    DisMunNum != 1L & 

    LocMunNum != 1L & 

    MainPlNum != 1L & 

    ProvNA  == 0L, 

  CommonLocality := GeoLevel[1]]

irp5[, CommonLocality := factor(CommonLocality, levels = c(GeoLevel, "none"))]

irp5[, NARowsMai := as.integer(sum(is.na(Mai))), by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

irp5[, NARowsLoc := as.integer(sum(is.na(Loc))), by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

irp5[, NARowsDis := as.integer(sum(is.na(Dis))), by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

irp5[, NARowsPrv := as.integer(sum(is.na(Prv))), by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

irp5[, TotalRowsInTxrf := as.integer(.N), by = .(Txrf, taxyear)]

qsave(irp5, paste0(pathdata, "irp5_LocationGranularity.qs"), 

  nthreads = DTThreads)



unique(irp5[Txrf %in% Txrf[grepl("^M", LocGranularFirm) | grepl("^M", CommonLocality)][1]

  & taxyear == 2009, 

  c("Txrf", "UID", geovars, grepout("Num|Rows|Gran|Commo", colnames(irp5))), with = F])
Txrf UID busprov_geo busdistmuni_geo buslocmuni_geo busmainplc_geo Num LocGranularFirm LocGranularJob MainPlNum LocMunNum DisMunNum ProvNum CommonLocality NARowsMai NARowsLoc NARowsDis NARowsPrv TotalRowsInTxrf

#### LocGranularFirm: the most granular location info at firm level

#### LocGranularJob : the most granular location info at each job level

#### MainPlNum      : the number of unique entries at main place level for firm-taxyear

#### NARowsMai      : the number of NA entries at main place level for firm-taxyear

#### CommonLocality : Lowest area level common among all the jobs for firm-taxyear

####   E.g., if all jobs are in Stellenbosch (main place), it is main place

####   If there are jobs attached to a branch in Capetown, then it is province

####   If location info is missing in some rows, then "none"



#### We know that rows with no location info can be allocated to the area level by:

####   MainPlNum == 1L                    ==> main place

####   MainPlNum >  1L & LocMunNum == 1L  ==> local muni

####   LocMunNum >  1L & DisMunNum == 1L  ==> district muni

####   DisMunNum >  1L & ProvNum == 1L    ==> province 



#### Panel of various levels

#### Main place panel: 

####  a. location entries are complete & location is granular down to main place level, or,

####        NARowsMai == 0L & LocGranularFirm == Mai

####  b. location == NA for some rows & uniquely common locality level is main place

####        NARowsMai > 0L  & MainPlNum == 1L

#### Local municipality panel: (addition to main place panel)

####  a. location entries are complete down to local municipality & location is granular down to local municipality, or,

####        NARowsLoc == 0L & LocGranularFirm == Loc

####  b. location == NA for some rows & uniquely common locality level is local municipality

####        NARowsLoc > 0L  & LocMunNum == 1L

#### District municipality panel: (addition to local municipality panel)

####  a. location entries are complete down to district municipality & location is granular down to district municipality, or,

####        NARowsDis == 0L & LocGranularFirm == Dis

####  b. location == NA for some rows & uniquely common locality level is district municipality

####        NARowsDis > 0L  & DisMunNum == 1L

#### Province panel: (addition to district municipality panel)

####  a. location entries are complete down to province & location is granular down to province, or,

####        NARowsPrv == 0L & LocGranularFirm == Prv

####  b. location == NA for some rows & uniquely common locality level is province

####        NARowsPrv > 0L  & ProvNum == 1L

iiM <- irp5[,

  NARowsMai == 0L & grepl("^M", LocGranularFirm) | 

  NARowsMai >  0L & MainPlNum == 1L]

iiL <- !iiM & irp5[, 

  NARowsLoc == 0L & grepl("^L", LocGranularFirm) | 

  NARowsLoc >  0L & LocMunNum == 1L]

iiD <- !iiM & !iiL & irp5[, 

  NARowsDis == 0L & grepl("^D", LocGranularFirm) | 

  NARowsDis >  0L & DisMunNum == 1L]

iiP <- !iiM & !iiL & !iiD & irp5[, 

  NARowsPrv == 0L & grepl("^P", LocGranularFirm) | 

  NARowsPrv >  0L & ProvNum   == 1L]

Compute HHI

We use main place level sample irp5M by following the “same sample data sets”

irp5M data only holds entries with location information of Main Place

  1. location entries are complete & location is granular down to main place level, or, NARowsMai == 0L & LocGranularFirm == Mai
  2. location == NA for some rows & uniquely common locality level is main place

For Jan 23, 2026, we use irp5[busmainplc_geo != "", ], which is close to irp5M, because I do not have time to run to get irp5M

#### code "hhi"

library(qs)

library(data.table)

if (!exists("irp5L")) rm(irp5L)

if (!exists("irp5D")) rm(irp5D)

if (!exists("irp5P")) rm(irp5P)

  irp5M <- qread(paste0(pathdata, "irp5M.qs"), 

    nthreads = DTThreads, use_alt_rep = TRUE)

used (Mb) gc trigger (Mb) max used (Mb)
Ncells 2.860563e+08 1.527710e+04 7.821635e+08 4.177210e+04 2.885715e+08 1.541140e+04
Vcells 2.636933e+10 2.011820e+05 3.809878e+10 2.906707e+05 2.645721e+10 2.018525e+05 
#### irp5 <- qread(paste0(pathsaveddata, "irp5.qs"), nthreads = 13)

geovars <- grepout("^bus.*geo$", colnames(irp5M))

GeoLevel <- c("Prv", "Dis", "Loc", "Mai")

LS <- NULL

for (yr in 8:22) {

  ipyr <- irp5M[taxyear == 2000+yr, ]

####  ipyr <- irp5[taxyear == 2000+yr, ]

  LShare <- NULL

  for (g in 1:length(GeoLevel)) {

    print(GeoLevel[g])

    byvar <- 	c("busprov_geo", "busdistmuni_geo", 

                "buslocmuni_geo", "busmainplc_geo")[1:g]

    #### ipGeo = copy(ipyr)

    #### CLAUDE rdn: copy() redundant — ipyr is already a copy from irp5[taxyear==yr];

    ####   the filter below creates another new table via <-; no := ever touches ipyr.

    #### ipGeo <- ipGeo[get(byvar[g]) != "" & !is.na(get(byvar[g])) & get(byvar[g]) != "EXCEPTION", ]

    #### CLAUDE rdn:

    #### Drop rows with no location info and location is "EXCEPTION"

    ipGeo <- ipyr[get(byvar[g]) != "" & !is.na(get(byvar[g])) & get(byvar[g]) != "EXCEPTION", ]

    #### ipGeo <- ipGeo[!grepl("none", LocGranularJob) & get(byvar[g]) != "EXCEPTION", ]

    print(summary(ipGeo[, grepout("NARows", colnames(ipGeo)), with = F]))

    ipGeo[, WorkersAtEstab := as.integer(.N), by = c(byvar, "Txrf")]

    #### ggplot(ipGeo[Txrf %in% Txrf[WorkersAtEstab > 50000][2], .(Txrf, a3601_income)], aes(x=a3601_income)) +

    #### geom_density() + scale_x_continuous(trans="log10") ==> # of workers > 50000 at main place seems correct

    #### E.g., Toyota in Umhlanga, Durban had over 80K workers    

	  #### Some firms have missing location information in some (but not all) rows

	  #### Cannot allocate 8 workers with missing location info between branches

    ipGeo[, firm:= as.integer(as.factor(Txrf))]

    ipGeo[, branch:= as.integer(as.factor(payereferenceno))]

    ipGeo[, ind:= as.integer(as.factor(UID))]

    if (yr == 12) print(ipGeo[Txrf %in% unique(Txrf[busmainplc_geo != ""])[2], 

        c(geovars, "firm", "branch", "ind",  "dateofbirth", 

        "periodemployedfrom", "WorkersAtEstab", "kerr_income"), with = F])

    ipGeo[, WorkersInMarket := as.integer(.N), by = byvar]

    ipGeo[, Share := round(WorkersAtEstab/WorkersInMarket, 8)]

    #### ShareG, HHIG: Share and HHI after dropping GovEntity ####

    ipGeo[, WorkersAtEstabNoGov := 0L]

    ipGeo[!grepl("gov", Entity), WorkersAtEstabNoGov := WorkersAtEstab]

	  #### Below gives private firm totals for rows of private firms

    ipGeo[, WorkersInMarketNoGov := as.integer(.N), by = c(byvar, "Entity")]

    ipGeo[, ShareNoGov := round(WorkersAtEstabNoGov/WorkersInMarketNoGov, 8)]

    ipGeo[, AreaLevel := GeoLevel[g]]

	  #### employment shares

    lshare <- unique(ipGeo[, c(

        byvar, "Entity", "Txrf",  "taxrefno",

        "AreaLevel", grepout("^Wo|^Sha", colnames(ipGeo))

      ), with = F])

    print(summary(lshare))

    lshare[WorkersAtEstab > 50000, ] 

    #### Shoprite-Checkers has HQ in Brackenfell, 160K employes

    #### MultiChoice (DSTV, etc.) has HW in Randburg, 8K employees

    #### PicknPay has HQ in Kinilworth (Main Place = Capetown), 90K employees

    #### Woolworth has HQ in Capetown, 33K employees

    lshare[, HHI := sum(Share^(2), na.rm = T), by = byvar]

    lshare[, nHHI := (HHI-1/WorkersInMarket)/(1-1/WorkersInMarket)]

    lshare[, HHIG := sum(ShareNoGov^(2), na.rm = T), by = byvar]

    lshare[, nHHIG := (HHIG-1/WorkersInMarketNoGov)/(1-1/WorkersInMarketNoGov)]

    LShare <- rbindlist(list(LShare, lshare), use.names = T, fill = T)

  }

  LShare[, AreaLevel := factor(AreaLevel, levels = GeoLevel)]

  LShare[, (geovars) := lapply(.SD, factor), .SDcols = geovars]

  setkey(LShare, AreaLevel, Txrf, busprov_geo, busdistmuni_geo, 

    buslocmuni_geo, busmainplc_geo, HHI)

  setcolorder(LShare, c("Txrf", "taxrefno", 

    "busprov_geo", "busdistmuni_geo", "buslocmuni_geo", "busmainplc_geo",

    "Entity", "AreaLevel", 

    "WorkersAtEstab", "WorkersInMarket", 

    "WorkersAtEstabNoGov", "WorkersInMarketNoGov"))

  LShare[, taxyear := 2000+yr]

  if (yr < 10) yr <- paste0("0", yr)

  qsave(LShare, paste0(pathdata, "ShareHHI", yr, ".qs"), nthreads = DTThreads)

  LS <- rbindlist(list(LS, LShare))

}

Plot HHI

library(ggplot2)

LS <- qread(paste0(pathsaveddata, "LShareHHI.qs"), 

  nthreads = DTThreads, use_alt_rep = TRUE)

gsh <- ggplot(data = LS, 

    aes(x = HHI)) +

  geom_density(alpha = .2) + 

  scale_fill_manual(values = "blue") +

  scale_color_manual(values = "blue") +

  scale_x_continuous(trans = "log10") +

  theme(legend.position = "none") +

  facet_wrap( ~ taxyear, dir = "tl")

ggsave(

  paste0(pathresults, "HHI2010-2022.jpg")

  , gsh, width = 8*2, height = 4*2, units = "cm",

  dpi = 300

 )
